Atalanta fugiens (Michael Maier, 1618)
================
Maier's 1618 alchemical emblem book — 50 emblems with epigrams, discourses, and musical fugues; the Rosicrucian era's most famous alchemical art-text. In Latin. Public domain. Source: Internet Archive (https://archive.org/details/atalantafugiensh00maie).

Getty  Research  Institute
